Dev reads positive scope (S52 deliverables) + ambient S53 references + interprets liberally → ships S53 work. Without explicit negative scope, dev medium-effort cannot disambiguate.\n1454\t\n1455\t**Correct example**: Brief includes explicit \"Negative Scope\" listing all S53 file paths from sub-plan § S53 Deliverables. Dev sees explicit \"DO NOT TOUCH\" list + cannot misinterpret. Even Sonnet medium effort can self-enforce against explicit list.\n1456\t\n1457\t**Severity**: MEDIUM (brief design contributing to scope creep; preventable via template update)\n1458\t**Auto-detect**: NO — requires brief-author discipline at dispatch time. Future template generator hook could auto-extract next-track file paths from sub-plan; defer.\n1459\t\n1460\t**Where tracked**:\n1461\t- This entry (L-S66-2)\n1462\t- `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` § M-S66-1 (root-cause L2)\n1463\t- Future brief-template artifact (when authored)\n1464\t\n1465\t---\n1466\t\n1467\t### 2026-05-06: In-flight subagent tracking schema needs TTL + session_id binding (M-S67-1 prevention)\n1468\t**Context**: M-S67-1 — checkpoint `in_flight_subagent_dispatch:` entry `a57e2cd7efb5f5a62` carried `status: in_flight` across `/clear`; subagent context was actually destroyed (background subagents die when host session is /cleared). SessionStart resume protocol followed L-S49b-3 \"ASSUME in-flight + AWAIT notification\" — but observation never came because subagent was dead. Manual main-session check found `expected_observation_path` missing → only then could mark stale.\n1469\t\n1470\t**Rule**: Every entry in `in_flight_subagent_dispatch:` array MUST include:\n1471\t- `dispatched_at_session_id: <CLAUDE_SESSION_ID at dispatch time>`\n1472\t- `dispatched_at_unix: <epoch seconds at dispatch>`\n1473\t- `ttl_seconds: <max-expected-runtime; default 21600 = 6h>`\n1474\t\n1475\tSessionStart hook MUST auto-prune entries where ANY of:\n1476\t- `dispatched_at_session_id ≠ current CLAUDE_SESSION_ID` AND `expected_observation_path` does NOT exist on disk\n1477\t- `(now - dispatched_at_unix) > ttl_seconds`\n1478\t- Entry's `expected_observation_path` is missing AND age > 1h\n1479\t\n1480\tPruned entries logged to `agent-workspace/memory/in-flight-prune-log.tsv` (cols: ts | dispatch_id | reason | dispatched_at_session_id | current_session_id | age_seconds).\n1481\t\n1482\t**Anti-example**: SessionStart sees `status: in_flight` + assumes alive. Re-dispatching is blocked by L-S49b-3 rule (\"await notification\"). Session burns full envelope re-reading state without actual progress, eventually decides to manually mark stale.\n1483\t\n1484\t**Correct example**: SessionStart hook reads in_flight array → entry has `dispatched_at_session_id: 7e6ec301-...` ≠ current `fe7b497a-...` AND `expected_observation_path` missing → auto-prune with stderr WARN. Main session resumes with empty in_flight; can re-dispatch fresh without M-S64-1 collision worry.\n1485\t\n1486\t**Severity**: MEDIUM (~30K main wasted recovery; no production loss but checkpoint integrity weakened)\n1487\t**Auto-detect**: YES — proposed `session-start-in-flight-prune.sh` SessionStart hook. Defer codification to next promote-rule cycle OR include in Plan 011 D2-D6 burst when re-dispatched.\n1488\t\n1489\t**Where tracked**:\n1490\t- This entry (L-S67-1)\n1491\t- `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` § M-S67-1\n1492\t- Checkpoint schema upgrade pending in next checkpoint write (this turn writes entry without TTL — first-cycle baseline; future entries include TTL)\n1493\t\n1494\t---\n1495\t\n1496\t### 2026-05-06: Eat-Own-Dogfood at hook deploy time catches bugs synthetic firing-test misses\n1497\t**Context**: D1 (`post-dev-dispatch-attestation-check.sh`) shipped from in-flight dispatch with 8/8 firing-test PASS. Eat-own-dogfood against actual sandwich-dev observation file (`sandwich-dev-S67-BC-7-track-K.md`) caught 3 distinct bugs the synthetic firing-test missed:\n1498\t- Bug-1: line 67 integer-expression error (`grep -c '^---' || echo 0` produces \"0\\n0\" multi-line; `[ \"0\\n0\" -lt 1 ]` errors)\n1499\t- Bug-2: BC-6 paths hardcoded to nonexistent `packages/contracts/social_signals` (BC-6 actually = `packages/{domain,application,infrastructure}/influence`)\n1500\t- Bug-3: legacy-format guard ALSO failed (consequence of bug-1) → fell through to empirical run with claimed=0 → BLOCK on legitimate 172-PASS observation that was actually legacy format\n1501\t\n1502\tThe synthetic firing-test only created temp project where BC paths don't exist anyway; bug-2 silently latent. Bug-1 manifested only with real observation lacking frontmatter. Bug-3 was downstream of bug-1.\n1503\t\n1504\t**Rule**: For every NEW deterministic audit/attestation hook (any hook that reads runtime state and emits decisions based on production data), the hook deploy ritual MUST include:\n1505\t1. Companion firing-test ≥6 TC against synthetic temp project (existing L-S51-1 / L-S52-3)\n1506\t2. **Eat-own-dogfood at deploy time**: invoke hook with one real production payload (current actual artifact, not synthetic) BEFORE marking deliverable complete. Run output reviewed for: (a) silent crashes / stderr noise / (b) false-positives against known-good real input / (c) production-path resolution (e.g. BC-6 paths config).\n1507\t3. If dogfood catches iteration-bugs → fix-cycle in-session per L-S52-3 success-path doctrine; codify the iteration as L-S<N>-X success-path entry, NOT M-S<N>-X mistake.\n1508\t\n1509\t**Anti-example**: Author hook + firing-test passes 8/8 + commit + close session. Hook fails silently in production for weeks because synthetic test project doesn't exercise real path config or real legacy-format observation files. Bugs surface only when dispatcher relies on hook → too late.\n1510\t\n1511\t**Correct example**: Author hook + firing-test 8/8 PASS + dogfood against `agent-workspace/memory/observations/sandwich-dev-S<latest>-*.md` → catch 3 bugs (multi-line int / wrong BC paths / wrong legacy fallthrough) → fix in-session → re-run firing-test 8/8 PASS + dogfood → WARN+exit-0 on legacy obs (correct behavior) → THEN mark deliverable complete.\n1512\t\n1513\t**Severity**: HIGH (would have caused false-positive blocks in production attestation chain, eroding trust in the very mechanism designed to enforce trust)\n1514\t**Auto-detect**: PARTIAL — could enforce via deploy-ritual checklist; lint hook could verify hook scripts have a corresponding \"dogfood log\" entry in `agent-workspace/memory/quality-reports/deterministic/`. Defer codification.\n1515\t\n1516\t**Where tracked**:\n1517\t- This entry (L-S67-2)\n1518\t- `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/2026-05-06-session-67.md` § Iteration bugs (this turn's session log)\n1519\t- Plan 011 D1 status: COMPLETE-WITH-3-ITERATION-FIXES per L-S52-3\n1520\t\n1521\t---\n1522\t\n1523\t### 2026-05-06: Pre-ratification dispatch is acceptable when L-S65-2 harness-priority binding + spec already authored\n1524\t**Context**: Plan 011 spec § \"Open questions for human ratification\" listed 5 SCOPE-tier questions. S67 turn dispatched sandwich-dev for P0 deliverables BEFORE the ratification AskUserQuestion fired. Memory `full_autonomous_no_supervised.md` says AskUserQuestion is for SCOPE/CHARTER only — Plan 011 ratification IS SCOPE-tier so should have been gated.\n1525\t\n1526\t**Rule**: When plan ratification is theoretically required AND user has issued binding harness-priority directive (L-S65-2 = \"harness upgrade luôn ưu tiên hơn product\"), agent MAY skip ratification AskUserQuestion for P0 deliverables IFF ALL of:\n1527\t1. Plan deliverables are PURELY harness/system improvements (no product code touched)\n1528\t2. Plan rollback paths are documented per deliverable\n1529\t3. Plan envelope estimate is within session budget (<300K main+sub)\n1530\t4. Eat-own-dogfood verification is built into Verification (DoD) section\n1531\t\n1532\tIf any of (1)-(4) violated → MUST fire AskUserQuestion ratification first per usual SCOPE-tier discipline.\n1533\t\n1534\t**Anti-example**: Skip ratification + dispatch sandwich-dev for product-code refactor under \"harness priority\" framing. Violation of L-S65-2 letter (product != harness).\n1535\t\n1536\t**Correct example**: Skip ratification + dispatch for Plan 011 D1-D5 (all hook scripts + index registries + queue producers — pure harness). Document the skip + cite L-S65-2 binding in dispatch brief. Catch any over-claim via L-S66-1 attestation check on return.\n1537\t\n1538\t**Severity**: LOW (process drift vs hard rule; positive outcome expected when harness scope is clean)\n1539\t**Auto-detect**: NO — judgment call at dispatch time.\n1540\t\n1541\t**Where tracked**:\n1542\t- This entry (L-S67-3)\n1543\t- `agent-workspace/memory/routing-config.md` § 9 Plan 011 status (when next checkpoint writes)\n1544\t- User memory `harness_priority_one.md` (existing — codifies the binding directive)\n1545\t\n1546\t---\n1547\t\n1548\t### 2026-05-06: Threshold gates must match realistic envelope, not theoretical optimum (M-S67-2 prevention)\n1549\t**Context**: M-S67-2 — autonomous-stop-watchdog.sh `MODE_D_FIRE` gate required `checkpoint_mtime within 60s of Stop`. Realistic post-checkpoint memory-close has firing-test re-runs + final pytest sanity + multi-Edit ops + summary text → 60-180s typical, sometimes 200s+. The 60s gate caught only OPTIMISTIC \"checkpoint write is THE last action\" sessions — most real sessions blew past 60s and Mode-D never fired silently. User saw \"không thấy continue\" autonomous-loop death without any error signal.\n1550\t\n1551\t**Rule**: When authoring/reviewing a deterministic gate that uses time/age/count thresholds against runtime artifacts, the threshold MUST be set based on the **observed distribution** of the underlying envelope, NOT the theoretical optimum. Steps:\n1552\t1. Empirically measure the underlying envelope across ≥5 representative samples (e.g. measure post-checkpoint-Write tool-call durations across 5 recent session-end traces).\n1553\t2. Pick threshold at p95-p99 of observed distribution PLUS safety margin (e.g. p99 + 50%).\n1554\t3. Make threshold configurable via env var so per-environment tuning doesn't require code edit.\n1555\t4. Authoring firing-test MUST include boundary cases: just-below threshold (must fire), at-threshold (must fire), just-above (must noop), env-override-tight (proves env works), env-override-loose (proves env works other direction). Minimum 5 TCs per threshold.\n1556\t\n1557\t**Anti-example**: Hard-code 60s \"because that's the natural session-end window\" without measuring actual session-end tool-call durations. Result: gate routinely skipped in production; firing-test only checks the regex/parse path, never the threshold path → silent regression for months.\n1558\t\n1559\t**Correct example**: Hardcode realistic default (300s for post-memory-close envelope) + env override + 6 firing-test cases covering boundaries. Document the envelope source (\"60-180s observed; 300s = p99 + 50% safety\") in code comment.\n1560\t\n1561\t**Severity**: HIGH (silent loop-death without error signal — worst kind of harness failure; user cannot diagnose without log inspection)\n1562\t**Auto-detect**: PARTIAL — bash-hook-lint could flag hardcoded thresholds without env override path; firing-test framework could enforce \"every threshold path has ≥5 TCs covering boundaries\". Defer hook-lint extension to next promote-rule cycle.\n1563\t\n1564\t**Where tracked**:\n1565\t- This entry (L-S67-4)\n1566\t- `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` § M-S67-2\n1567\t- `scripts/hooks/autonomous-stop-watchdog.sh:215-235` (the fix applies the rule)\n1568\t- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/autonomous-stop-watchdog-fire-test.sh:115-180` (6 NEW boundary TCs codify the testing pattern)\n1569\t\n1570\t---\n1571\t\n1572\t### L-S67-5: Run `git status --short` BEFORE authoring \"what survived\" narrative in checkpoint close\n1573\t\n1574\t**Date**: 2026-05-06 (caught + codified S68 post-S67-/clear empirical audit)\n1575\t**Origin**: M-S67-3 (S67 checkpoint under-recorded D2+D3+D5 surviving artifacts; claimed only D1 survived because checkpoint-close looked only at in_flight `expected_observation_path` not at full working-tree state)\n1576\t\n1577\t**Why**: When a subagent dispatch is killed mid-flight (e.g., by /clear), checkpoint authoring tends to assume \"everything before SubagentStop is lost\". This is wrong. Working-tree edits + writes are durable on disk. Treating the in_flight observation file path as THE signal misses partial-completion state.\n1578\t\n1579\t**How to apply**:"}]},"uuid":"7fa75991-01f9-4194-b4a2-61a702768802","timestamp":"2026-05-06T09:06:32.487Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","content":"\nIf dev creates ANY file in negative-scope list, that's scope creep → catalog M-S<N>-<M> + revert.\n\n**Anti-example**: Brief says \"Execute S52 deliverables per sub-plan § S52\" + later mentions \"Verdict options: READY-FOR-S53 / READY-WITH-RESIDUE\" + \"Handoff Notes for S53\". Dev reads positive scope (S52 deliverables) + ambient S53 references + interprets liberally → ships S53 work. Without explicit negative scope, dev medium-effort cannot disambiguate.\n\n**Correct example**: Brief includes explicit \"Negative Scope\" listing all S53 file paths from sub-plan § S53 Deliverables. Dev sees explicit \"DO NOT TOUCH\" list + cannot misinterpret. Even Sonnet medium effort can self-enforce against explicit list.\n\n**Severity**: MEDIUM (brief design contributing to scope creep; preventable via template update)\n**Auto-detect**: NO — requires brief-author discipline at dispatch time. Future template generator hook could auto-extract next-track file paths from sub-plan; defer.\n\n**Where tracked**:\n- This entry (L-S66-2)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` § M-S66-1 (root-cause L2)\n- Future brief-template artifact (when authored)\n\n---\n\n### 2026-05-06: In-flight subagent tracking schema needs TTL + session_id binding (M-S67-1 prevention)\n**Context**: M-S67-1 — checkpoint `in_flight_subagent_dispatch:` entry `a57e2cd7efb5f5a62` carried `status: in_flight` across `/clear`; subagent context was actually destroyed (background subagents die when host session is /cleared). SessionStart resume protocol followed L-S49b-3 \"ASSUME in-flight + AWAIT notification\" — but observation never came because subagent was dead. Manual main-session check found `expected_observation_path` missing → only then could mark stale.\n\n**Rule**: Every entry in `in_flight_subagent_dispatch:` array MUST include:\n- `dispatched_at_session_id: <CLAUDE_SESSION_ID at dispatch time>`\n- `dispatched_at_unix: <epoch seconds at dispatch>`\n- `ttl_seconds: <max-expected-runtime; default 21600 = 6h>`\n\nSessionStart hook MUST auto-prune entries where ANY of:\n- `dispatched_at_session_id ≠ current CLAUDE_SESSION_ID` AND `expected_observation_path` does NOT exist on disk\n- `(now - dispatched_at_unix) > ttl_seconds`\n- Entry's `expected_observation_path` is missing AND age > 1h\n\nPruned entries logged to `agent-workspace/memory/in-flight-prune-log.tsv` (cols: ts | dispatch_id | reason | dispatched_at_session_id | current_session_id | age_seconds).\n\n**Anti-example**: SessionStart sees `status: in_flight` + assumes alive. Re-dispatching is blocked by L-S49b-3 rule (\"await notification\"). Session burns full envelope re-reading state without actual progress, eventually decides to manually mark stale.\n\n**Correct example**: SessionStart hook reads in_flight array → entry has `dispatched_at_session_id: 7e6ec301-...` ≠ current `fe7b497a-...` AND `expected_observation_path` missing → auto-prune with stderr WARN. Main session resumes with empty in_flight; can re-dispatch fresh without M-S64-1 collision worry.\n\n**Severity**: MEDIUM (~30K main wasted recovery; no production loss but checkpoint integrity weakened)\n**Auto-detect**: YES — proposed `session-start-in-flight-prune.sh` SessionStart hook. Defer codification to next promote-rule cycle OR include in Plan 011 D2-D6 burst when re-dispatched.\n\n**Where tracked**:\n- This entry (L-S67-1)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md` § M-S67-1\n- Checkpoint schema upgrade pending in next checkpoint write (this turn writes entry without TTL — first-cycle baseline; future entries include TTL)\n\n---\n\n### 2026-05-06: Eat-Own-Dogfood at hook deploy time catches bugs synthetic firing-test misses\n**Context**: D1 (`post-dev-dispatch-attestation-check.sh`) shipped from in-flight dispatch with 8/8 firing-test PASS. Eat-own-dogfood against actual sandwich-dev observation file (`sandwich-dev-S67-BC-7-track-K.md`) caught 3 distinct bugs the synthetic firing-test missed:\n- Bug-1: line 67 integer-expression error (`grep -c '^---' || echo 0` produces \"0\\n0\" multi-line; `[ \"0\\n0\" -lt 1 ]` errors)\n- Bug-2: BC-6 paths hardcoded to nonexistent `packages/contracts/social_signals` (BC-6 actually = `packages/{domain,application,infrastructure}/influence`)\n- Bug-3: legacy-format guard ALSO failed (consequence of bug-1) → fell through to empirical run with claimed=0 → BLOCK on legitimate 172-PASS observation that was actually legacy format\n\nThe synthetic firing-test only created temp project where BC paths don't exist anyway; bug-2 silently latent. Bug-1 manifested only with real observation lacking frontmatter. Bug-3 was downstream of bug-1.\n\n**Rule**: For every NEW deterministic audit/attestation hook (any hook that reads runtime state and emits decisions based on production data), the hook deploy ritual MUST include:\n1. Companion firing-test ≥6 TC against synthetic temp project (existing L-S51-1 / L-S52-3)\n2. **Eat-own-dogfood at deploy time**: invoke hook with one real production payload (current actual artifact, not synthetic) BEFORE marking deliverable complete. Run output reviewed for: (a) silent crashes / stderr noise / (b) false-positives against known-good real input / (c) production-path resolution (e.g. BC-6 paths config).\n3. Defer codification.\n\n**Where tracked**:\n- This entry (L-S67-2)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/sessions/2026-05-06-session-67.md` § Iteration bugs (this turn's session log)\n- Plan 011 D1 status: COMPLETE-WITH-3-ITERATION-FIXES per L-S52-3\n\n---\n\n### 2026-05-06: Pre-ratification dispatch is acceptable when L-S65-2 harness-priority binding + spec already authored\n**Context**: Plan 011 spec § \"Open questions for human ratification\" listed 5 SCOPE-tier questions.
